=========================================

  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,

  `lng` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `backoff` varchar(1) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `email` varchar(35) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,

  `ctddate` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdtime` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdupd` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `polda_id` varchar(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `polres_id` varchar(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,

) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=47 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================

  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=9 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================
TABLE: smd_tbl_data_detail_irsms
=========================================
CREATE TABLE `smd_tbl_data_detail_irsms` (
  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `rowid` bigint NOT NULL,
  `no_laka` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `nik` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jenis_kelamin` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `alamat` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `tanggal_lahir` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `usia` bigint DEFAULT NULL,
  `pendidikan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `pekerjaan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jenis_sim` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jenis_kendaraan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `tipe_kendaraan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdby` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctddate` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdupd` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `nama` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jenis_korban` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdtime` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_delete` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_valid` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_server` varchar(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `last_updated` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`pk_ids`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=61805 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================
CREATE TABLE `smd_tbl_data_integrasi` (
  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `rowid` varchar(36) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `jenis_data` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `tanggal` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `polda` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `polres` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kategori` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `sub_kategori` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `jumlah` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_server` varchar(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `last_updated` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`pk_ids`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=151246 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================
TABLE: smd_tbl_data_irsms
=========================================
CREATE TABLE `smd_tbl_data_irsms` (
  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `rowid` bigint NOT NULL,
  `no_laka` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `polda` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `polres` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `tanggal_kejadian` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `tingkat_kecelakaan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jumlah_md` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `jumlah_lb` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `jumlah_lr` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `koordinat_gps_lintang` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `koordinat_gps_bujur` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `titik_acuan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `jarak_lokasi_kecelakaan` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `arah_lokasi_kecelakaan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `informasi_khusus` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `tipe_kecelakaan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kondisi_cahaya` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `cuaca` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kecelakaan_menonjol` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `nama_jalan` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `fungsi_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kelas_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `tipe_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kemiringan_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `status_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `bentuk_geometri` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `kondisi_permukaan_jalan` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `batas_kecepatan_lokasi` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `kerugian_material_non_kendaraan` bigint DEFAULT NULL,
  `kerugian_material_kendaraan` bigint DEFAULT NULL,
  `keterangan_kerugian` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `ctdby` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctddate` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdupd` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `ctdtime` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `waktu_kejadian` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` datetime(6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_delete` varchar(225) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_valid` int DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_server` varchar(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `last_updated` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`pk_ids`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=34532 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================
TABLE: smd_tbl_data_traffic_counting
=========================================
CREATE TABLE `smd_tbl_data_traffic_counting` (
  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `polda` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `polres` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `nama_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `jenis_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `status_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `category` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `keterangan` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `tanggal` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `jam` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `bus` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `minibus` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `truck` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `motor` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `total` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_server` varchar(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `last_updated` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`pk_ids`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=39034 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

=========================================
TABLE: smd_tbl_data_traffic_counting_history
=========================================
CREATE TABLE `smd_tbl_data_traffic_counting_history` (
  `pk_ids` bigint unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `polda` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `polres` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `nama_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `jenis_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `status_cctv` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `category` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `keterangan` longtext COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci,
  `tanggal` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `jam` time DEFAULT NULL,
  `bus` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `minibus` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `truck` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `motor` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `total` decimal(20,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_server` varchar(50) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `last_updated` datetime NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`pk_ids`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uq_pk_source` (`pk_ids`,`source_server`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=42166 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
